## Common questions

### Where can I find YC Startup jobs?

This list has 300 open YC Startup roles from 161 companies, refreshed every 2 days from landed's live job database. Each role links to a landed job page with the employer's own application.

### Which companies are hiring for YC Startup roles right now?

Top-rated companies hiring now include David AI, Legora, Reducto, HappyRobot, Tennr, Numeral, Avoca AI and Salient. 300 roles are at YC-backed companies.

### Are there remote YC Startup jobs?

Yes. 84 of the 300 roles are remote, marked 🌐 in the list.

### Are there entry-level YC Startup jobs?

Yes. 13 roles are internships or entry level.

### How can I apply to more of these, faster?

[landed](https://app.landed.jobs/signup?from=github&cta=github-list-bulk&item=yc-startup-jobs&utm_source=github&utm_medium=referral&utm_campaign=github-yc-startup-jobs&utm_content=faq) matches the roles here to your resume and applies for you, and Enzo drafts your answers for you to review.

<details>
<summary><b>How this list is built</b></summary>

This list is generated from landed's live job database and refreshed every two days. It keeps open roles posted in the last 90 days that link to an employer application. Only roles at Y Combinator companies (by YC batch in the company record) in the SF Bay Area, New York, or remote are kept, grouped by place and then by batch, newest batches first. Each company appears at most 5 times, and staffing agencies are left out. Company badges come from TrueUp and Y Combinator data.

Spotted a closed or wrong role? [Open an issue](https://github.com/landedjobs/yc-startup-jobs/issues/new).

</details>
